# Skills · R · part 128

843 entries, review-advisors to review-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-advisors**: Spawn a cast of contrarian advisors who independently review a document, design, PR, or RFC from complementary dimensions. Each advisor runs as a parallel sub-agent and produces a structured review. The host synthesizes all reviews into a Review Report — a structured artifact designed as input to /review-synthesizers. Use when you want adversarial multi-perspective critique before shipping, merging, or committing to a design. Trigger phrases: "review advisors", "contrarian review", "adversarial review", "multi-perspective review", "stress test this". ([VledicFranco/fractal-co-design](https://github.com/VledicFranco/fractal-co-design/tree/HEAD/skills/_support/review-advisors))

- **review-agent-failed-to-apply-its-ignor-ecafde6175b90bf0**: Fix for review agents that post findings on vendored, generated, or third-party code despite an ignore list. Use when vendor or generated files get comments, the ignore config exists but findings still appear, or runs waste budget on files nobody owns. Key triggers: findings in vendor/, node_modules, or generated files; ignore config present but ineffective. Not for disagreements about whether a file should be ignored. - **review-agent-failed-to-cache-the-diff-d2ce9d512b2d2472**: Fixes a review agent re-fetching the PR diff on every retry by caching the diff keyed on PR number and head SHA, and reusing it across retries. - **review-agent-failed-to-calibrate-sever-8b82ef8c2e524a77**: Fix for review agents that label every finding critical, which trains reviewers to ignore the label entirely. Use when severity distributions skew to the top, trivial nits ship as critical, or reviewers complain everything is on fire. Key triggers: all-critical runs, style nits marked critical, severity ignored by reviewers. Not for PRs that genuinely contain multiple critical issues. - **review-agent-failed-to-catch-a-real-sq-62638df40f9b9a72**: Debugs a review agent missing a real SQL injection by reproducing the miss with a test case, finding the gap in its detection rules, and adding the missing pattern. Use when the reviewer overlooks a known-bad security construct in the diff. Triggers: missed injections, silent dangerous patterns. Not for false positives, or vulnerability classes outside the agent's checklist. - **review-agent-failed-to-check-resolved-2f3c65696d42005a**: Fix for review agents that open a new review thread on a topic a human already discussed and resolved. Key triggers: duplicate threads on the same lines, resolved threads re-litigated, parallel conversations on one issue. Not for duplicate findings within a single review run. - **review-agent-failed-to-dedupe-double-p-a274124fa87506f6**: Fixes a review agent posting duplicate summary comments on retry by checking for its existing comment before posting, and updating in place instead. - **review-agent-failed-to-detect-a-draft-b89303d96602e9bc**: Fix for review agents that request changes on draft PRs, which cannot be merged anyway and where early verdicts just create noise. Key triggers: draft PRs receiving formal verdicts. Not for informational comments on drafts, which are fine.
